# GodSim building art report

**23 November 2026, branch `art/buildings`.** The catalog covers all 75 building types, five races and ten ages. The work list supplies 935 building entries and 23 wonder entries. Its age bands expand to 50 individually modelled Town Centers, 100 houses, 875 other buildings and nine wonders. Each race gets two houses per age. The four shared wonders use neutral models with bindings for all five races.

## Verification record

- Every GLB contains three serialized LODs and one material. The 1,024 × 1,024 atlas and 256 × 256 emissive mask use 666,384 bytes under the configured iPhone ASTC 6×6 format, including both complete mip chains. The 2,096 previews include 2,026 work-list appearances and 70 earlier playable race-specific unlocks derived from the content reference.
- Independent package critics recorded 1,706 model evaluations over 180 package rounds. Among the 1,034 final evaluations, the lowest scores were 91 for silhouette, 90 for race and age identity, 91 for construction, 90 for charm, 90 for colour and 99 for technical health. Zero defects remain open. The integration gate checks the current GLB, every age preview, the textures, shader and material against each review. Package authorship was distributed across coordinator sessions, dedicated builders and the lead. Some builders owned more than one package. Critics were independent of the models they scored.
- In the first full-catalog comparison, the art director held 34 models across 16 functions. Corrective work included separate Dwarf coal and ore-washing plants, mechanized farms, emergency entrances for three races and broad age-responsive roof, yard or gate surfaces. All 34 passed re-review. The director approved all 19 comparative pages, and all 19 canonical PNGs matched those hashes. A complete publication now requires that the current models, previews, shared resources and family sheets match the independent approval. [Cross-race findings](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/art_direction.md) · [Approval and fingerprints](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/art_direction_approval.json)
- All 15 AssetForge unit tests passed. They cover shared textures and emission, serialized UVs and geometry, the complete source-to-runtime mapping, age and variant assignment, stale or low-scoring reviews, director holds and sign-off, contact-page retirement and fresh rebuild outputs. An independent check substituted a mismatched family-page hash and confirmed that both complete publication paths rejected it. The sample generator also rebuilt byte-identically on this host for its three GLBs, three previews, fragment and two textures.
- A survey of every exported LOD checked for exposed same-facing coplanar overlaps and inward closed shells. All 1,062 adjacent-age preview pairs had different pixels in the initial full survey. Comparing adjacent ages at 64 pixels found changes too subtle to read, which led to the subsequent visual repairs. The surface survey and critics' observations document the extent of the geometry checks. [Geometry survey](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/surface_checks.json)
- GLB attributes use little-endian float32. Manifest bounds come from those serialized positions, removing the source double-precision discrepancy seen in the original sample. A frozen, earlier set of all 71 generators rebuilt 3,209 files identically in two runs. Later inventories grew to include the assembled catalog and supplemental contact sheets. The final full-catalog comparison uses the approved art and includes nested package sheets. Tests were conducted on this Apple M1 using the supplied virtual environment. Reproduction on a second machine was not tested.
- **Both complete signed builds matched across 4,905 files**, with zero SHA-256 differences. Each pass ran all 71 generators, required every production GLB and age preview to be rewritten and admitted 1,034 models, 2,130 runtime bindings and 280 canonical pages. The inventories include supplemental contact images, including 46 nested sheets, and retained package review images. The inventory digest is `5182d6604c2060843a4f8d9ca2a0fbab77d57531af8c62c1f8e827783e83aa8a`. [First-pass inventory](/Volumes/BattleRoomEmpire_SSD/chatgpt/codex/godsim-building-art/verification/signed-catalog-CEjR4mtM/pass-1.json) · [Second-pass inventory](/Volumes/BattleRoomEmpire_SSD/chatgpt/codex/godsim-building-art/verification/signed-catalog-CEjR4mtM/pass-2.json) · [Complete build log](/Volumes/BattleRoomEmpire_SSD/chatgpt/codex/godsim-building-art/verification/signed-catalog-CEjR4mtM/build.log)
- The Unity renderer and importer compiled against the installed Unity 6000.0.38f1 managed assemblies. The UnityEditor stub check also compiled. All **19 headless integration tests passed** on the complete catalog. The 200-building test read LOD0 from 200 distinct GLBs, checked the transformed vertices and normals, and combined 435,072 vertices and 183,314 triangles into four single-material body meshes. Three used 32-bit indices. This measures body batching in the test harness. Driver draw calls including shadows, banners and construction effects require an actual player capture. [Independent diff review](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/final_diff.md)
- The independent final reviewer closed all seven diff findings, reconstructed the expected inventory, rehashed all 4,905 files and reconciled the 1,706 score rows with the individual records. The final binary audit covered 1,756,063 triangles across 3,102 LODs and found no discrepancies in the tested geometry, UVs, texture references, budgets or runtime bindings. [Final disposition](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/final_diff.md#final-disposition)
- The compatibility check scanned 380 unchanged Core files with zero errors or warnings. The standard solution build stopped before tests because the pinned Microsoft.NET.Test.Sdk 17.11.1 was unavailable offline. Using the cached 17.12.0 host, the full simulation suite ran twice with **3,023 passed, 24 skipped and three failed** each time. `FamineRecovery_SettlementStarvesThenRecoversWithin15Years` observed zero starvation deaths. `TownsGrowSensibly_After60Years` found excess housing and missing civic buildings. `HousingRule_OccupancyAndHomelessnessStayWithinDesignBands` found occupancy outside its design range. The test inputs match the base commit. [Full NUnit result](/Volumes/BattleRoomEmpire_SSD/chatgpt/codex/godsim-building-art/verification/GodSim-full.trx)
- Unity Editor import, real glTFast mesh import, GPU shader compilation and iPhone visual or performance testing were not run. The checkout specifies 6000.0.23f1 and the offline machine supplied 6000.0.38f1 managed assemblies. Headless tests use software rendering or managed test fixtures.
- The working copy started at `2c7e33a`. Git staging was blocked when the sandbox denied creation of `.git/index.lock`. The changes remain in the working tree for coordinator review. No push or pull request was made.

## Scope and output

958 work-list entries cover 75 building types. The plan requires 1,034 distinct meshes and 2,096 model/age previews. Counts include two house variants per race and age. Each racial and shared wonder has one mesh.

1,034 models have a GLB and fragment on disk. 1,034 have a current passing independent review. 1,034 are in the runtime manifest. 71 of 71 complete packages clear both review and surface checks.

| [dwarf_wonders](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/dwarf_wonders.json) | 1 | `bld_dwarf_deep_forge_ancient_v0` | 94 | 92 | 85 | 90 | 92 | 96 | The four brass crossbars on the two front buttresses hover in LOD0. At their centre z positions of -0.954 and -0.714, the stone incline is about 0.198 and 0.446 tiles high, while the bar centres are 0.290 and 0.550. Seat the underside of each bar in the slope across its width and inspect a low oblique view for daylight. / At LOD0 and LOD1 the three stock ingots straddle the right-hand buttress edge at x=0.775. They read as short horizontal fittings sticking out of its side. Move the entire pile onto clear, level ground beside the buttress, with the top ingot resting across the two lower ingots. Retain a clear operator route. / The open, dark flues in LOD0 and LOD1 turn into two solid orange disks in LOD2. The low mesh fills the top rim at full height with the forge patch. Preserve a recessed dark inner face or dark cap inside the low-poly rim, then compare all three meshes from above so the pair of stacks remains visually continuous. |
